Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on relativity structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 633. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 75 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the relativity space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 633 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 75. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^837(S, F) is trivial. QED.
